# Skills · F · part 29

847 entries, film-production to finalize-stack. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **film-production**: Run a film or series end to end from a rough outline: develop the premise into a screenplay and series bible, lock characters, world and visual style, build the shot list, storyboards and animatic, generate shots on local models overnight while the user sleeps, edit, sound, finish, QC and deliver. Use when the user says "make a movie", "turn this idea into a film", "create a series", "produce this script end to end", "I have an outline, make the whole thing", "make this overnight", or asks for the full production pipeline rather than one clip or one prompt. Local-only by default: LTX-2.3 join… ([nyimbi/nyimbi-skills](https://github.com/nyimbi/nyimbi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/film-production))

- **film-repack**: 适用于把一支 30 分钟以上的纪录片、访谈或长演讲（B 站 b23.tv/BV、YouTube、本地 MP4，含英文等非中文原声）重做成三件套：带原片时间码的逐字稿、可核对的中文压缩总结、带原片静帧的 PPTX 课件，以及用 motion-showreel 渲染的 1920×1080 口播 MP4。触发场景：用户说「下载这个视频并总结」「做成 PPT」「做成口播成片 / HTS MP4」，或直接贴链接加片长。不用于中文讲解短视频的一条命令成片（用 video-to-deck-reel）、多集课程（course-repack）、只要下载文件、已有成片只做剪辑。 ([yhyu13/AgentSkillsVault](https://github.com/yhyu13/AgentSkillsVault/tree/HEAD/SKILL/video/tier0/film-repack) · ★3)
